    GUI = graphical user interface (the thing you interact with instead of the command line or terminal. It’s got buttons and stuff you actually click to do whatever you want to do)
sidecars = additional helper scripts to do expand on the app because it doesn’t explicitly provide options to do some of the stuff that I want it to do, which the program it’s interfacing for allow. And I can’t modify the actual program because it’s all been compiled and assembled and I don’t which files I’d even begin to fuck with even if I could edit them
